# FC02 — Munkres mapping

Status: **Sweep II complete for FC02.** All 844 canonical units C01–C14 are audited below.

## Per-row provenance codes

- `P`: pinned Mathlib `db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d` (v4.33.0), current upstream `4cbb42e75a050e830b7cf0f2ae748d7644f59cf7` rechecked 2026-09-05; Apache-2.0.
- `N`: project + pinned/current Mathlib + Loogle/LeanSearch + Reservoir/GitHub Lean + source-atlas negative search, 2026-09-05.
- `J`, `I`, `R`: project-existing, package-import, reference-port respectively; any such row retains its exact target/license details in the route/target/comparison fields.

## Provenance contract for C01

- Project search: `LeanCategories/**` outside `.lake` was searched before external reuse; no project-owned replacement for Munkres §§1–11 was found. `LeanCategories/Foundation/Sets.lean` packages finite/countable categories but does not own the textbook C01 statements.
- Pinned Mathlib: repository pin `db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d` (`v4.33.0`) was searched and declaration-level interfaces were checked for sets, relations, functions, finite/countable types, orders, ordinals, choice, recursion, and Zorn.
- Current Mathlib: current default branch rechecked 2026-09-05; the latest inspected commit was `251e86bd1fa42024826ccdb46c3d680edff03e1f`. Open PR #41962 packages the `ω₁` counterexample space but is not needed for the C01 order-theoretic mapping because the pinned library already contains the required `ω₁`/regularity declarations.
- External Lean search: GitHub/Lean ecosystem searches found `mdnestor/basic_topology@10230d7997436e8be1644d0750ec6b03d845619b`, but its Munkres sequence begins at §12 and therefore supplies no C01 owner; the repository also exposes no license file, so it would not be a `reference-port` source in any case. Exercise-solution repositories were not credited because the frozen corpus explicitly excludes exercises.
- Search-service cross-checks: Loogle and LeanSearch were queried for countability/union and adjacent foundational interfaces; LeanSearch returned `Set.Countable.sUnion` and `Set.countable_iUnion`.
- `exact match` means the assertion/interface is present up to harmless notation or a canonical reindexing; `broad match` means the mathematics exists in a more structural/native interface; `partial match / wrong interface` means a substantive packaging or semantic clause differs; `unmatched after search` is scoped to the searches above.
- Mathlib/Lean code is Apache-2.0. No external C01 package was credited.

## Provenance contract for C02

- Project search: `LeanCategories/**` outside `.lake` was searched first. Specialized topology uses exist (adic completions, quotient manifolds, adelic spaces), but no project-owned declaration replaces the Munkres §§12–22 foundational units, so none is credited merely for importing/using the same Mathlib interfaces.
- Pinned Mathlib: repository pin `db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d` (`v4.33.0`) was declaration-checked across topology definitions, bases, order topology, products/Pi types, subspaces, separation, continuous maps, metric/metrizable spaces, uniform convergence, and quotient maps.
- Current Mathlib: default branch rechecked 2026-09-05 at `4cbb42e75a050e830b7cf0f2ae748d7644f59cf7`; no current-only declaration was needed to upgrade a C02 verdict.
- External Lean search: GitHub/ecosystem/source-atlas searches rechecked `mdnestor/basic_topology@10230d7997436e8be1644d0750ec6b03d845619b`, whose Munkres files cover these sections, but the repository exposes no license file. It is therefore search corroboration only, not a `package-import` or `reference-port` route. Loogle/LeanSearch, Reservoir-style package discovery, broad GitHub Lean search, and the local formalization source atlas produced no separately licensed C02 owner that improved the routes below.
- The arbitrary Pi **product topology** is native in Mathlib (`continuous_pi_iff`, `isTopologicalBasis_pi`). The pinned and importable `Counterexamples/SorgenfreyLine.lean` supplies the lower-limit/Sorgenfrey topology itself and several of its standard separation/countability facts. No searched bundled **box topology**, K-topology, or Munkres uniform topology was found; concrete textbook examples are not counted as implemented merely because general definitions could be instantiated to prove them.
- `exact match`, `broad match`, `partial match / wrong interface`, and `unmatched after search` retain the C01 meanings. Mathlib/Lean code is Apache-2.0.

## Provenance contract for C03

- Project search: `LeanCategories/**` outside `.lake` was searched first; no project-owned declaration supersedes the foundational connectedness/compactness/local-compactness interfaces below.
- Pinned Mathlib: repository pin `db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d` (`v4.33.0`) was declaration-checked across connectedness, path components, order topology/intermediate value, compactness, metric compactness, sequential/countable compactness, local compactness, and one-point compactification.
- Current Mathlib: default branch rechecked 2026-09-05 at `251e86bd1fa42024826ccdb46c3d680edff03e1f`; no current-only declaration was needed to upgrade a C03 verdict.
- External search: Loogle/LeanSearch, Reservoir/GitHub Lean, the local source atlas, and web search were checked for the concrete ordered-square, topologist’s-sine-curve, ordinal, and minimal-uncountable-well-order examples. `mdnestor/basic_topology@10230d7997436e8be1644d0750ec6b03d845619b` remains unlicensed and therefore corroboration only, not a creditable route. No separately licensed formal owner improved the unmatched concrete-example rows.
- Concrete examples remain unmatched unless an existing theorem/declaration actually realizes them; generic infrastructure that could be instantiated in a new proof is not counted as implementation.
- Mathlib/Lean code is Apache-2.0; verdict vocabulary is unchanged from C01–C02.

## Provenance contract for C04

- Project-first search found no project-owned replacement for the foundational countability/separation/manifold units.
- Pinned Mathlib `db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d` (v4.33.0) and current upstream `4cbb42e75a050e830b7cf0f2ae748d7644f59cf7` were declaration-checked for bases/countability, Lindelöf/separable spaces, separation axioms, Urysohn/Tietze, partitions of unity, manifolds, and Whitney embedding.
- Loogle/LeanSearch, Reservoir/GitHub Lean, the source atlas, and 2026-09-05 web/GitHub searches found the pinned/importable `Counterexamples/SorgenfreyLine.lean` owner for the Sorgenfrey line/plane topology, first-countability, separability, complete normality, failure of second countability, and failure of normality of the plane. No creditable owner was found for the missing Sorgenfrey Lindelöf claims or for the K-topology, ordered-square, ordinal-product, or uncountable-product counterexamples. `mdnestor/basic_topology` remains unlicensed corroboration only.
- `P`/`N` provenance codes are as defined above; concrete examples are not credited merely because generic infrastructure could prove them.

## Provenance contract for C05

- Project-first, pinned/current Mathlib, ecosystem/GitHub/source-atlas and 2026-09-05 web searches were rechecked. Mathlib owns Tychonoff, ultrafilter maximal-extension machinery, and Stone–Čech with its universal property; no creditable Lean owner was found for the four concrete compactification examples.
- Mathlib’s `StoneCech` construction is ultrafilter/T2-quotient based, not Munkres’s product-of-intervals construction; construction-sensitive rows are marked partial rather than silently conflated. `P`/`N` are the provenance codes defined above.

Open PR #41676 at `cd6d319518617a42e975351cde7e6f20dac12512` proves Bing–Nagata–Smirnov and sigma-discrete refinements; PR #41719 at `cede174dcee855873dc8d81a7b998a07407e903c` proves the regular-space paracompact TFAE and regular-Lindelöf theorem; PR #41962 at `bdf021dde563eb64202fee8732b82066c5b2593d` supplies the ω₁ hereditary-paracompactness counterexample. - `mdnestor/basic_topology@10230d7997436e8be1644d0750ec6b03d845619b` has no corresponding §§39–42 implementation and remains unlicensed. `formal-conjectures` searches returned no relevant Nagata/Smirnov/paracompactness statements.

Negative rows are instead grounded in project, pinned/current/open-Mathlib, package, source-atlas, Reservoir/GitHub, and broad code searches.

## Provenance contract for C08

- Project search: `LeanCategories/**` outside `.lake` was searched first; no project-owned Baire/category or Lebesgue-covering-dimension development supersedes the interfaces below.
- Pinned Mathlib: `db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d` (`v4.33.0`, Apache-2.0) was declaration-checked for Baire spaces, Gδ/meagre/nowhere-dense sets, complete-metric and locally-compact Baire theorems, nested complete-set intersections, affine independence/affine spans, manifold embeddings, and the counterexample tree. `import Counterexamples.NowhereDifferentiable` compiles against this exact pin; therefore `Counterexamples/NowhereDifferentiable.lean` is credited as pinned `mathlib`, not as a reference-only source.
- Current/open Mathlib: default branch `4cbb42e75a050e830b7cf0f2ae748d7644f59cf7` and open PRs were searched on 2026-09-05. Mathlib has `SmallInductiveDimension` and a current zero-dimensional-space PR, but these are not Munkres’s Lebesgue covering dimension and are not used as substitutes. No open PR for the covering-dimension or Menger–Nöbeling statements was found.
- External search: `google-deepmind/formal-conjectures@8323e878b83fcd7f4a448256069352a265460d75` (Apache-2.0) defines `HasLebesgueCoveringDimensionLE/Eq` in `FormalConjecturesForMathlib/Topology/LebesgueCoveringDimension.lean` using finite open covers; GitHub-wide Lean searches found no further proved development of these predicates. That revision uses Lean `v4.33.1` and Mathlib `0df444a360eaa60ab8c11dca51a86af692955474`, whereas this project pins Mathlib `v4.33.0` at `db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d`; no direct package compatibility test was performed, so these definitions remain `reference-port`, not `package-import`. The finite-cover interface is equivalent to Munkres’s all-open-cover definition on compact spaces but not definitionally identical for arbitrary spaces. An `awesome_theorems` search hit concerned metric covering numbers, a different notion, and the repository has no detected license, so it is not credited.
- Broad searches found no proved Lean owner for Munkres’s Baire-one dense-continuity theorem, the density of nowhere-differentiable functions in `C([0,1],ℝ)`, the general-position perturbation lemma, or the covering-dimension embedding theorems.

## Provenance contract for C09

- Project search: `LeanCategories/**` outside `.lake` was searched first for homotopy, fundamental-group, covering-space, retract, fixed-point, sphere/projective-space, and van-Kampen declarations; no project-owned implementation supersedes the routes below.
- Pinned Mathlib: `db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d` (`v4.33.0`, Apache-2.0) was declaration-checked across `Topology/Homotopy`, `AlgebraicTopology/FundamentalGroupoid`, `Topology/Covering`, cyclic-group APIs, complex polynomial roots, radial sphere equivalences, and stereographic projection. A local compile probe used the explicit Lean `v4.33.0` toolchain to check the covering/fundamental-group interfaces.
- Current/open Mathlib: GitHub default branch was rechecked on 2026-09-05 at `4cbb42e75a050e830b7cf0f2ae748d7644f59cf7`; that exact object was fetched and searched. No current/open Mathlib owner was found for a circle `π₁ ≃ ℤ` theorem, Brouwer/Borsuk–Ulam, topological deformation retracts, Seifert–van Kampen for `π₁`, real-projective-space fundamental groups, or the surface computations below. Loogle’s live JSON search returned the existing fundamental-group declarations; the LeanSearch public endpoint requires POST and could not be exercised through the available connector policy, so no unmatched verdict relies on a LeanSearch-only negative.
- External/source-atlas search: `mrdouglasny/jacobian-challenge@8ce1ec28aed1c78b3b39d0bae26e2858c82f5143` (Apache-2.0, Lean/Mathlib v4.30.0 at Mathlib `c5ea00351c28e24afc9f0f84379aa41082b1188f`) supplies native-Mathlib monodromy bijectivity, punctured-plane `π₁ ≃ ℤ`, and Rado Kirov’s two-open van-Kampen corollary; `harfe/fixed-point-theorems-lean4@770940ddf9878cf61952ed53d910b92bca841838` (MIT, v4.32.0, Mathlib `81a5d257c8e410db227a6665ed08f64fea08e997`) supplies Brouwer; `mrdouglasny/spectral-positivity@9af3c3a7d9146c92a0ecae5ad269848bd4c25fca` (Apache-2.0, v4.30.0) supplies Perron–Frobenius. Those three remain revision-mismatched `reference-port` sources. `Arthur742Ramos/ComputationalPathsLean@f90943eecdde516817fbc33863486b681a0a9645` (MIT) now pins Lean v4.33.0 and this project’s exact Mathlib revision `db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d`; its genuine `AddCircle` topological circle `π₁` winding module was rechecked at that revision and `lake build ComputationalPaths.Path.CompPath.CircleTopologicalRealization` succeeds, so U041 is directly usable by `package-import`.
- Semantic rejection/negative evidence, dated 2026-09-05: broad GitHub/Reservoir/web searches found no completed Borsuk–Ulam or ham-sandwich owner, no genuine real-projective-space/topological figure-eight computation, and no full all-`n≥2` sphere-simply-connected theorem. The similarly named ComputationalPaths `RealProjective2` is literally a `PUnit` abbreviation, and its figure-eight free-group result is conditional on an unsupplied `HasWedgeSVKDecodeBijective`; those names are therefore not treated as implementations. Public Borsuk–Ulam benchmark hits are statements/axiomatized placeholders, not proofs.

## Provenance contract for C10

- Project search: `LeanCategories/**` outside `.lake` was searched first for Jordan separation, invariance of domain, planar-graph embeddings, winding number, and Cauchy-formula owners; no project-owned C10 declaration was found.
- Pinned Mathlib: repository pin `db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d` (`v4.33.0`, Apache-2.0) was checked declaration-by-declaration for stereographic projection, connected components, homotopy, finite graphs, and complex Cauchy integrals. It supplies infrastructure and the combinatorial `K_n`/`K_{m,n}` definitions, but no Jordan-curve, planar Kuratowski, or winding-number theorem. The open Mathlib issue #33018 still contains only a `sorry` statement for invariance of domain and is not credited as an implementation.
- Current Mathlib / search services: current default branch was rechecked 2026-09-05 at `4cbb42e75a050e830b7cf0f2ae748d7644f59cf7`. Live Loogle name searches returned no Jordan-curve or winding-number declaration; its `Kuratowski` hits are the unrelated metric-space embedding. Mathlib's current undergraduate coverage file still leaves winding number blank.

## Provenance contract for C11

- Project search: `LeanCategories/**` outside `.lake` was searched for direct-sum/free-product/free-group presentations and for van Kampen, wedges, cell attachment, torus, and dunce-cap owners. No project-owned C11 theorem or example was found.
- Pinned Mathlib: project pin `db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d` (`v4.33.0`, Apache-2.0) has substantial exact algebraic ownership: `DirectSum`, `Monoid.CoprodI`, `FreeGroup`, normal closure, commutators/abelianization, presentations, and the finitely generated abelian structure theorem. It has fundamental-group/path-class and product-groupoid infrastructure, but no topological van Kampen theorem, wedge-of-circles computation, 2-cell attachment theorem, torus fundamental-group computation, or dunce-cap formalization.
- Current Mathlib/open work: current default branch was rechecked 2026-09-05 at `4cbb42e75a050e830b7cf0f2ae748d7644f59cf7`. Open PR #41603, head `9a19745bf2565abbbf56bb38c91bcd26c4c53b49`, supplies a fundamental-groupoid/cosheaf van Kampen theorem but is unmerged and targets current upstream, so credited rows are `reference-port`, never pinned Mathlib. Open PR #41856, head `4a1f4b8bf1a32dd2734cd68d01db22f2af31bc62`, supplies `AddCircle.windingNumberIso : π₁(S¹) ≃ ℤ`; it is likewise `reference-port` and is not credited as implementing wedge/torus examples that it only makes derivable.
- External/source-atlas search: `mdnestor/basic_topology` has no C11 implementation. `mrdouglasny/jacobian-challenge@8ce1ec28aed1c78b3b39d0bae26e2858c82f5143` (Apache-2.0, Lean 4.30.0, Mathlib `c5ea00351c28e24afc9f0f84379aa41082b1188f`) contains the vendored Kirov two-open telescoping engine `KirovDolbeault/VanKampen.lean`, but its proved capstone is a simply-connectedness criterion rather than Munkres's group pushout/kernel theorem; it is not used as a false exact owner. `kadirhanpolat/pytop` has an additive-group `AmalgamDatum`/`Pushout` formalization but no topological π₁ van Kampen theorem, so it is not credited to the source theorem. An exact Munkres-oriented Isabelle development exists at `JUrban/isa_algtop1@37a45d805f4bb382f2ec4237ffe1b710c571f7e1` (including `Theorem_68_7_quotient_free_product`), but the repository exposes no LICENSE/README and uses Isabelle rather than the project's Lean toolchain; it is therefore recorded only as negative provenance evidence, not `reference-port`.
- Negative verdicts below are dated 2026-09-05 and cover project source, pinned/current Mathlib, open Mathlib work, Loogle/LeanSearch where queryable, Reservoir/GitHub Lean searches, and the known topology source-atlas candidates above. Concrete examples are not marked implemented merely because their ingredients are available.

## Provenance contract for C12

- Project search: `LeanCategories/**` outside `.lake/.agents/.git` was searched first for polygonal edge-pasting, surface-group, first-homology, polygon-word normalization, triangulation, and covering-space conventions; no project-owned C12 declaration was found.
- Pinned Mathlib: project pin `db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d` (Lean v4.33.0, Apache-2.0) was checked for polygons, presented groups, abelianization/free abelian groups, fundamental-group basepoint transport, connectivity/covering maps, and induced fundamental-group images. It directly owns the abstract `H₁ = π₁^ab` ingredients/convention and the covering-subgroup convention, but has no surface-specific polygonal quotient theorem, surface `π₁`/`H₁` computation, or surface-classification theorem.
- Current Mathlib / search services: current default branch was rechecked 2026-09-05 at `4cbb42e75a050e830b7cf0f2ae748d7644f59cf7`; open-PR, Loogle/LeanSearch, Reservoir, and GitHub Lean searches found no native surface fundamental-group/homology/classification owner filling the negative rows below.
- External/source-atlas search: `mccorvie/classification-of-surfaces@e3c7230fe78d7b056a415d9ecae6f77887046b32` is an Apache-2.0 formalization of compact-surface classification with polygonal quotients, signed cyclic presentations, P1/P2/cancellation moves, canonical orientable/nonorientable words, triangulation-to-polygonal realization, and complete normalization. It pins Lean v4.32.0 and Mathlib `81a5d257c8e410db227a6665ed08f64fea08e997`, differing from this project pin, so every credited declaration from it is `reference-port`, never `package-import`.
- Negative verdicts below are dated 2026-09-05 and cover project source, pinned/current Mathlib, open Mathlib work, indexed search services, and the dedicated external surface-classification source. Generic algebraic or quotient-space ingredients are not credited for concrete surface examples or computations unless an actual declaration realizes the source unit.

## Provenance contract for C13

- Project search: `LeanCategories/**` outside `.lake/.agents/.git` was searched first for covering-space classification, universal covers, deck groups, quotient actions, and semilocal simple connectivity; no project-owned C13 theorem/example was found.
- Pinned Mathlib: project pin `db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d` (Lean v4.33.0, Apache-2.0) owns the general covering-space lifting criterion, fundamental-group map/range infrastructure, subgroup conjugation/normalizers, orbit quotients, quotient-covering infrastructure, and the ambient definitions needed below. It does not yet contain the connected-cover classification/deck-group theorem suite.
- Current Mathlib: default branch rechecked 2026-09-05 at `4cbb42e75a050e830b7cf0f2ae748d7644f59cf7`; its new `Mathlib/Topology/Covering/Deck.lean` supplies the deck-transformation subgroup but is post-pin and therefore recorded only as a `reference-port`.
- Current Mathlib freshness recheck (2026-09-06): upstream `HEAD` is `810b3888d0aa94294b18587c453466bc86c1f0fc`; the `4cbb42e75a050e830b7cf0f2ae748d7644f59cf7..810b3888d0aa94294b18587c453466bc86c1f0fc` file delta contains no `Mathlib/Topology`, `Mathlib/AlgebraicTopology`, covering, graph, or free-group path, so the C13 current-Mathlib positive owner and negative searches are unchanged.
- External/source-atlas search: `Tau-Ceti/lean` at `907e560a48c8b8a8a74ad9d9a6871c2431939fed` (Apache-2.0, Lean `4.34.0-rc2`, Mathlib `5fcc6656691ed31965746c369f41fa75e567ac9d`) contains a substantial, source-shaped classification of connected covers, universal covers, monodromy, deck groups, regular covers, subgroup realizations, and semilocal simple connectivity. Its post-pin toolchain makes every credited TauCeti row `reference-port`, never `package-import`.
- TauCeti freshness recheck (2026-09-06): upstream `HEAD` is `f67536627686de753796221ae5a6d503061369cd`; the complete `907e560a48c8b8a8a74ad9d9a6871c2431939fed..f67536627686de753796221ae5a6d503061369cd` path delta is confined to analysis/PDE/Sobolev, Hecke-ring, and Huber-ring files, with no topology/covering/graph change. Lean `4.34.0-rc2` and Mathlib `5fcc6656691ed31965746c369f41fa75e567ac9d` are unchanged, so the dated C13 negative covering-space searches remain valid against current TauCeti HEAD.
- `Arthur742Ramos/ComputationalPathsLean@f90943eecdde516817fbc33863486b681a0a9645` is MIT and exactly matches this project's Lean/Mathlib pins, but its deeper covering-space APIs are bespoke computational-path data rather than Mathlib topological `IsCoveringMap`, so no C13 row is credited from it merely for compatibility.
- Negative verdicts below are dated 2026-09-05 and cover project source, pinned/current Mathlib, open/current work, TauCeti, ComputationalPaths, indexed search services, and the local source atlas. Concrete examples are not credited merely because their generic ingredients are formalized.

## Provenance contract for C14

- Project search: `LeanCategories/**` outside `.lake/.agents/.git` was searched first for linear/topological graph realizations, edge-path reduction, tree/fundamental-group results, Euler characteristic, and Schreier-index results; no project-owned C14 theorem or example was found.
- Pinned Mathlib: project pin `db584cd6d46c92f209a44c0f1c829460d327499d` (Lean v4.33.0, Apache-2.0) owns the combinatorial oriented-edge/walk API, trees and spanning-tree extension machinery, Nielsen–Schreier, and subgroup index/coset cardinality used below.
- Current Mathlib freshness recheck (2026-09-06): upstream `HEAD` is `810b3888d0aa94294b18587c453466bc86c1f0fc`. The complete file delta from the previously audited `4cbb42e75a050e830b7cf0f2ae748d7644f59cf7` contains no relevant `Topology`, `AlgebraicTopology`, quiver/simple-graph, free-group, Schreier, or subgroup-index path, so no C14 verdict is upgraded to current Mathlib.
- Historical Mathlib PR #11604 (`feat: Serre graphs, fundamental groups, covering spaces`) is **closed, unmerged** at head `0a7c341ccbe6b354f277b0f48bad76e13e6ea866` (Apache-2.0, Lean 4.7.0-rc2). Its `Mathlib/Combinatorics/Multigraph/ReducedPaths.lean` supplies the path-reduction construction used only as a revision-specific `reference-port` for U015.
- Open draft Mathlib PR #41603 (`WIP: ... Seifert–van Kampen`) remains open at head `9a19745bf2565abbbf56bb38c91bcd26c4c53b49` (Apache-2.0, Lean 4.32.0-rc1). Its `Mathlib/AlgebraicTopology/FundamentalGroupoid/VanKampen.lean::relative_vanKampen_subtype` is therefore recorded only as a revision-specific `reference-port` for U022, never as pinned Mathlib.
- `alonamaloh/schoenflies-lean` was rechecked at current `HEAD` `05a43d29cde026618777db3d4e4316204ccca237` (Apache-2.0, Lean 4.32.2, Mathlib `905b95818eb32af7874a58b427f50c1711a5e96c`). Its finite planar drawing formalization gives the geometric graph owners for U001 and U013, but the older toolchain and specialized plane/drawing hypotheses make them `reference-port` routes.
- `Arthur742Ramos/finite-graph-fundamental-group` was rechecked at current `HEAD` `dd57e3ab8bc5a7042fcc5498e778b008d87ac032` (Apache-2.0, Lean 4.32.0, Mathlib `81a5d257c8e410db227a6665ed08f64fea08e997`); `lake build FiniteGraphFreeGroup` succeeds at that revision. It supplies the topological graph-realization, edge-path, no-backtracking, tree-contraction, and graph fundamental-group interfaces below, but is a `reference-port` against this project's newer 4.33 pin.
- `Arthur742Ramos/GraphCoveringTheory` was rechecked at current `HEAD` `c918a72c5170503c98fd66a0f3116b7bfcd1250c` (Apache-2.0, Lean 4.32.0, Mathlib `81a5d257c8e410db227a6665ed08f64fea08e997`); `lake build GraphCoveringTheory` succeeds. It includes the finite-graph development and a sharp Schreier-index formalization, again as `reference-port` only.
- TauCeti was refreshed to upstream `HEAD` `f67536627686de753796221ae5a6d503061369cd`; the delta from audited `907e560a48c8b8a8a74ad9d9a6871c2431939fed` contains no topology/covering/graph change. ComputationalPaths remains at current `HEAD` `f90943eecdde516817fbc33863486b681a0a9645`; its compatible computational bouquet/path developments do not implement the missing Munkres linear-graph assertions. The unlicensed Lean-3 repository `dwarn/nielsen-schreier-lean@99fb30c0bf321c651edbb7524101604cf0242ea1` corroborates the sharp index formula but is not a creditable route because no license is present.
- Negative verdicts below are dated 2026-09-06 and cover project source, pinned/current Mathlib, current/open/historical PR work, TauCeti, ComputationalPaths, the two current Arthur742Ramos graph developments, Schoenflies, indexed search services, GitHub/Reservoir searches, and the local source atlas. Generic ingredients are not credited as realizing a concrete Munkres example or topology theorem unless a declaration actually supplies the relevant interface.
